**Board Status:**

**FEN representation** shows: board is set up with White to move now

**My Move Options:**

I see I'm Black and it's move 5. The board shows a normal setup opening. I should consider:

1. **Knight's move**: ...d6, ...e6 (Hexagonal/controlling center)
2. **Queen's move**: ...c5, ...d5 to challenge center
